Caught mid-transcription when the Physics agent found "Q46–50 Section B" beyond Q45; confirmed by reading p73 (ends Q200) + p20 (Q48–50 still Physics). **Fixed durably (TDD, +3 tests → 25):** added `questionCount` to the `Paper` config (200 for 2023/2024/Re-NEET-2024, 180 for 2025/2026); `allowedSubjectsForNumber(n, questionCount)` now derives the equal-quarter block boundaries from the count; `commit.ts` coverage check + `buildRecords` are count-aware. So 2024 + Re-NEET 2024 (both 200 q) run for free. **Ingestion:** the E3 code (73pp, single-column, WITH worked solutions — the E1 code is answer-key-only, 43pp, so pick a solved code) → 4 parallel vision agents (Phy 1–50 · Chem 51–100 · Bio 101–150 · Bio 151–200; the booklet is cleanly Botany 101–150 then Zoology 151–200) → commit PRIVATE (200/200 valid, 0 subject/chapter flags) → verify → 21 figures → flip PUBLIC. **Two source quirks handled:** (1) the E3 "PDF" is a **continuous-flow document whose page breaks BISECT text lines** (top half of a line on page N-1's bottom, bottom half on page N's top) → ~42 of 200 questions had a clipped opening line, faithfully reconstructed by the agents from the printed worked solution (spot-verified Q104 matches verbatim); (2) **5 questions had no explicit `Answer (N)` line** (it fell in the page-break gap) → recovered from the worked solution, which for NEET always concludes at the correct option (Q59→4, Q62→4, Q79→1 all re-verified). Q35 (physics, "none of the options correct") flipped PUBLIC with the official awarded answer (1*) + transparent note, per the preserve-defect convention. **The figure verify-gate earned its keep again:** I montage-reviewed all 21 crops and caught **5 defects the anchor-agents' self-verify missed** — 3 answer-leaks (Q65/Q87/Q95 cropped in the `Answer` line because `answerY` was set below it) + 2 clips (Q40 dipole labels, Q66 diazonium `N₂Cl`) — all re-cropped + re-verified before `flip-public` (gate passed). Reconfirms [[neet-ingestion]] + [[figure-snapcrop-verify]] (answerY is the one input geometry can't infer; montage-verify is mandatory). Code + data staged, not yet committed (per the user's review-each-paper cadence); NEET 2024 + Re-NEET 2024 next.
- **2026-07-07 — State Board Ch.6 Line and Planes COMPLETE + PUBLIC (134 q; 9th Class-12 Maths chapter — ALL of Part-01 now done) + a live-regression fix to the `/board` chapter index.** The direct application of Ch.5 Vectors to 3-D lines/planes, through the proven `scripts/stateboard/` pipeline: config → **6 parallel vision agents** (§6.1 / §6.2-6.3 / Misc 6A / §6.4 / §6.5-6.7 / Misc 6B) → merge (134 q, 0 dup refs) → commit PRIVATE (33 solved + 81 exercise-subjective + 20 MCQ across 7 subtopics) → **20 MCQ keys blind-re-derived** (19/20 matched the official key) → **4 parallel solution-authoring agents** (81 subjective) → `apply-solutions.ts`. UNUSUAL book layout (faithfully preserved in `sections.ts`): only 4 numbered exercises + TWO Miscellaneous blocks — 6A sits MID-chapter (after Ex 6.2), 6B is terminal; exercise numbers are OFFSET from section numbers (Ex 6.2 covers §6.2+§6.3, Ex 6.3 = §6.4, Ex 6.4 = §6.5-6.7). **Answer-key cross-check vs the Part-1 ANSWERS section (rendered p271-274) found 0 genuine bank errors vs 5 book defects** — all flagged `[Textbook…]`: Misc I Q.5 (defective options — the correct SD `5/√26≈0.98` matches none of the four printed options; official key D retained to match the board), Misc I Q.18 (wrong key — the two lines are actually SKEW (STP=240≠0) so no plane contains both; option A is the plane through line 1 with normal `b₁×b₂=(17,-47,-24)`, B's normal isn't ⟂ to line 1, so A not B), Misc A Q.8 (book's direction (1,1,1) isn't ⟂ to `b=(1,2,1)` (dot=4); correct is `b×c∝(1,-1,1)`), Misc A Q.11 (book λ=−10/11 doesn't zero the DR dot product; correct λ=70/11 — the well-known NCERT value), Ex 6.4 Q.2 (`·`-for-`=` line misprint). Also **fixed 1 our-incompleteness** — Misc II Q.12 "equal intercepts" has a second (degenerate all-zero-intercept) plane `x−2y+z=0` besides `x+y+z=6`; appended it. The cross-check ran as 3 our-vs-book agents AFTER a **dump-script bug** (the dump emitted only a `has_solution` boolean, not the solution text) made a first agent pass compare against empty solutions — caught it, re-dumped with `solution`, re-ran; lesson: verify the cross-check input actually carries our answer before trusting an all-AGREE result. **No `solution_image`** (abstract 3-D coordinate geometry — same call as Vectors). `board:lint` clean (134 q, 14 blocks); flipped 134 PUBLIC; `order_index` 1-7. **The `/board` smoke-test surfaced a live regression** (permission-gated fix, user-approved): `listBoardChapters` did a raw un-paged `.select()` and PostgREST's 1000-row cap silently DROPPED whole chapters from the `/board/[examSlug]` index once MH-HSC-12's backfilled rows crossed 1000 (during yesterday's Vectors ingest → 1,301) — trig, LP AND the new chapter were missing. Fixed by range-paginating in 1000-row windows (the documented in-repo remedy; identical to the `quiz:lint` fix). All 9 chapters now list; verified live. `npm run stats` → MH-HSC-12 = 1,435. Reconfirms [[state-board-ingestion]] + [[textbook-chapter-ingestion]] + [[audit-probe-symmetry]] + [[board-reader]] + the **PostgREST 1000-row cap** pitfall.
- **2026-07-06 — State Board Ch.5 Vectors COMPLETE + PUBLIC (235 q; 8th Class-12 Maths chapter, on `/board`) — the largest State Board chapter yet (61pp) and the deepest answer-key cross-check to date.** 5 subtopics (Vectors & Types / Section Formula / Dot Product / Cross Product / Scalar & Vector Triple Product), through the proven `scripts/stateboard/` pipeline: config → **6 parallel vision agents** (5.1–5.5 + Miscellaneous) → 2 small gap-fill fragments hand-authored after agents flagged page-boundary gaps (Ex 5.1 Q9–15 on p19, Ex 5.3 Q12–14 on p37, both correctly identified by neighboring agents as out-of-range but nobody's assigned range covered them) → merge (235 q, 0 dup refs) → commit PRIVATE → **20 MCQ keys independently re-derived blind** (19/20 matched) → **5 parallel solution-authoring agents** (146 exercise-subjective). **The mandatory answer-key cross-check (step 6) earned its keep twice over:** (1) caught my OWN over-correction — I'd first "fixed" a duplicate-printed-option MCQ (Misc I Q.4, both B and C printed as π/4) by editing option C to π/3, but the book's own Miscellaneous-5 answer table names **B** as correct — re-fixed to match; (2) a **figure-reading fix** on Ex 5.1 Q.6 (Fig 5.34): my first transcription had `\vec{QP}=a` per an ambiguous arrowhead read, giving `c=(b-a)/2,d=(a+b)/2` — the book's key showed `c=(a+b)/2,d=(b-a)/2`, exactly swapped; re-examining the actual figure image showed the arrow for "a" points toward Q (`\vec{PQ}=a`, not `\vec{QP}=a`) — fixing that one arrow direction made our answer an **exact match**, not just a relabeling, proving it was our transcription error, not the book's. Cross-checked all 5 exercises + Misc II via 6 parallel agents (each independently re-deriving every disagreement, never trusting either side): **found 2 genuine bank errors** (Ex 5.3 Q8ii — a stem symbol confusion, p̂·r̂ should have been q̂·r̂; Q13 — direction ratios are sign-ambiguous, missed the second valid point) fixed via `subjectiveContentHash`-safe stem+solution updates, vs **10 book-key errors** (2 sign typos in Ex 5.1 Q2/Q3 — one exposed by the book's own OTHER answers being self-inconsistent with it; an incomplete answer dropping the ±120° branch; a second-root typo in a direction-cosine system that fails the given equations entirely; a dropped ×5 magnitude scale; a decimal-point typo `1.6`→`16`; a value exactly half the correct cross-product-associativity result (verified 2 independent ways); an internally-inconsistent parallelogram-diagonal answer; and — the most interesting catch — **a "show that" identity that is mathematically FALSE as printed** (`Misc II Q.35`: the claimed RHS `2a×c` — verified the transcription faithful against the source image, then proved by direct expansion the LHS is identically **zero** for any vectors, never `2a×c`)). All 10 flagged `[Textbook…]` — but the bracket must sit at the START of `solution` (not the end, which is where I first put it) for `errata.ts`'s `startsWith("[")` scan to pick it up; caught via a "why isn't this showing in the errata report" check, fixed with a small regex-based batch move. `errata.ts` now spans **~69 items / 8 chapters**. **No `solution_image`** — vector algebra is abstract enough that diagrams were flagged as "would help intuition" (hexagons, medians, tetrahedra) but never blocking, unlike Linear Programming's feasible regions or Application-of-Integration's area regions. `board:lint` clean (235 q, 12 blocks); `/board/mh-hsc-12/mathematics/vectors` + `npm run stats` (1,301 MH-HSC-12 total) both live-verified. Reconfirms [[state-board-ingestion]] + [[textbook-chapter-ingestion]] + [[audit-probe-symmetry]] + [[board-reader]].

A textbook chapter is 3 buckets: **solved examples** (worked, WITH the book's `Solution:` → ship PUBLIC), **exercise MCQs** (the "Select the correct answer" block → answer DERIVED), and **exercise subjective** (free-response Q.N → i)/ii)/iii… → answer pending). Board PYQs are a later phase under the same exam. State in [[state-board-ingestion]]; reusable how-to in [[textbook-chapter-ingestion]].
  - **The load-bearing design call: model subjective questions as a first-class format, not an MCQ subset** (user chose "both"). Migration 0041 adds `question_format` enum (`mcq`|`subjective`, default `mcq` → every existing row + MCQ path untouched). A subjective question = a `questions` row with **zero option rows**, its model answer in `solution`. App branches (all TDD, +18 tests): `subjectiveContentHash(stem, context)` (namespaced `SUBJECTIVE\n` prefix so it can't collide with an MCQ hash; hashes `context` so set-siblings with the same bare sub-item text don't dedup-collide; excludes `solution` so answer-backfill is id-stable); `commitStaged` writes the format + emits no options for subjective; `QuestionRow`/`/browse` QuestionCard render "Show model answer" (no A–D); the docx answer-key prints the model answer (not `(?)`). Deferred (nothing subjective reaches them): format-filter toggle, subjective edit-form, quiz/RAG guards.
  - **Set-grouping is the defining textbook shape.** A textbook `Q.N` instruction followed by sub-items `i) ii) iii)…` becomes the bank-native SET model: the shared instruction → `context`, each sub-item → its own row, siblings share `setLabel` (commit turns it into `set_id`). So `Q.1 (i–xv)` = 15 sibling rows, not one blob and not 15 context-less fragments.
  - **Extraction is a HYBRID: text for stems/options/prose, VISION for truth tables + figures.** Mathematical Logic's solutions are almost all truth tables, and the PDF text layer serializes table cells into unusable flat `T T F T…` runs — so tables are transcribed by **vision agents → GFM pipe-tables** (`parseTableBlocks` renders them). The 6 teaching sections (1.1–1.5 + the Miscellaneous MCQ+subjective block) were fanned out to **6 parallel vision agents** (one per section, ref-prefixed to keep refs unique), each writing a `data/logic-12.<sec>.json` fragment → `merge.ts` → `commit.ts`. Unicode logic symbols (∧∨→↔∼∀∃) → LaTeX in `\(…\)`.
  - **Ship rule: PUBLIC iff the question has a trustworthy answer.** `flip-public.ts` flips only the solved examples (`question_format='subjective' AND solution IS NOT NULL` — a clean data-driven selector: exercise-subjective has null solution, MCQ is excluded). Result: **237 rows committed PRIVATE practice → 61 solved examples flipped PUBLIC** (book-authoritative worked solutions, truth-table pipe-tables intact) + 169 subjective exercises + 7 derived-answer MCQs staged PRIVATE for a review/answer-authoring pass. ~35 switching-circuit **figure-only** questions (§1.5 + Misc Q.12–17) were deferred to a figure-attach pass (agents transcribed the symbolic/text ones, skipped pure-diagram ones). - **2026-07-01 — NEET figure crops re-tooled + all 68 figures re-verified leak-free (pure geometry `scripts/lib/figures/snapcrop.py` + pure helpers `scripts/lib/figures/verify.ts` [both promoted from `scripts/neet/` on 2026-07-02 so the other figure pipelines can adopt them — recipe in `scripts/lib/figures/README.md`] + the neet CLIs `scripts/neet/snap-crop.ts` + `verify-figures.ts` + a `flip-public` figure-verify gate; 21 tests in `tests/figures-verify.test.ts`).** The ingestion's eyeballed fractional bboxes had leaked answers/options into figures at scale — a `/dashboard/reports` batch flagged 5 NEET-2026 Physics figures, and a full audit found **9 of 10** 2026-Physics figures defective (answer-leaks like Q5/Q18/Q39 literally showing "Answer (N)"; Q27 cropped the *wrong* figure region). Replaced the eyeball with **snapCrop**: coarse anchors — a rough column band + two whitespace-gap anchors (top below the stem, bottom above the options) + an **`answerY` ceiling** — that PyMuPDF turns into a tight bbox by bounding the page ink, guarding `fy1 < answerY` so the answer can't be cropped in. `verify-figures.ts` emits a contact-sheet HTML + montage PNG + a per-paper verdict file, and `flip-public` now **refuses PUBLIC until every figure is reviewed-`ok`** (un-skippable, replacing the manual montage-verify that had been skipped). Swept **all 68 figures across 2025 (23) + 2026 (18) + Re-NEET (27)** — every paper now verified-ok, ~35 re-cropped, and **10 answer-leaks total surfaced** (5 more were hiding among "option-bleeds" the compressed review montages had obscured). Then a follow-up **stem-vs-image cross-check** (2026-07-02; 8 parallel agents, each reading a batch of stems + reading the crops, checking every element the question text NAMES — points P/Q/A/B, axis labels, option counts — is actually in the crop) caught **6 clipped stem-named labels** the "looks complete" eyeball pass had missed (Re-NEET Q10 "P" + Q16 "A"; 2025 Q7 "P", Q23 option curves, Q115 plasmid markers, Q126 "B") — all re-cropped. So the figures are now clean on BOTH axes: answer-leak (nothing below `answerY`) and completeness (every named label present). The "what to look for" half of that cross-check was then **automated** as `extractStemLabels(stem)` in `verify.ts` (deterministic: subscripts `L_1`/`D_2`, geometry runs `ABCD`, prose "point(s)/labels: X, Y", `List I/II`, option-counts; 8 TDD cases) and **wired into the contact sheet** so every figure card prints a "✓ confirm present in crop: `P` `Q` …" checklist — a review aid (presence-confirmation still needs vision), not a gate. Durable lessons (answerY is the one load-bearing input geometry can't infer → verify-gate mandatory; two-column papers need column-scoped bands; triage-before-recrop; **the reliable completeness check is textual not visual — cross-check the stem's named elements**) in [[figure-snapcrop-verify]] + the [[neet-ingestion]] figure section. Commits `832f801`→`ea4fb62` (branch `neet-figure-snapcrop` merged to main, full prepush green each push). **NOTE (scope assessed 2026-07-02):** NEET was the *unique* high-risk pipeline — pure-scanned pages with hand-eyeballed page-band bboxes. A data+code audit of the others found them structurally safer: MHT-CET (246 figs) + JEE (118) upload pandoc-extracted `media/` files verbatim (no crop → leak-free by construction); Foundation (283) crops **unioned raster image-blocks** (answer text isn't a raster block → answer-leak structurally unlikely); CDS has 0 figures. So the eyeballed-page-band failure mode doesn't exist in the embedded-object/block-union pipelines — the gate's forward value is at the *next scanned-booklet ingest*, not a retroactive retrofit. The shared core is import-ready for that; a bounded Foundation clipped-label spot-check is logged as a permission-gated backfill candidate (SUGGESTIONS.md ledger).

- **2026-07-01 — NEW exam: NEET (UG) — 539 q PUBLIC across 3 papers (2025, 2026, Re-NEET 2026) via a new `scripts/neet/` vision pipeline. First scanned-booklet exam WITH official keys + worked solutions.** Source: Allen-style "Answers & Solutions" booklets under `C:\tmp\PYQPs\NEET\` — image PDFs (no text layer) but each of the 180 questions carries the stem, four options `(1)-(4)`, an official **Answer (N)** / **Ans. (N)**, and a full worked **Sol.** (with diagrams). The multiple booklet CODES per exam are the same question set reshuffled (seat randomization) → **one clean single-column code ingested per exam** (2025 code 45, 2026 code 11, Re-NEET code 50). State + how-to in [[neet-ingestion]].
  - **Setup (Phase 0):** seeded the `NEET` exam (`01d193e7-…`) + **4 subjects Physics · Chemistry · Botany · Zoology** (direct INSERT; NEET officially splits Biology into Botany+Zoology) and registered the `neet` slug in `examContext.ts` (exam picker + `/browse?examId` + `/notes/neet` hub auto-derive; 3 negative exam-context tests that used "neet" as a stand-in unknown slug were repointed to `ipmat`/`cuet` + positive NEET assertions added). Seeded a **canonical NCERT Class 11–12 chapter catalog** (`NEET_CHAPTERS`: Physics 28 · Chemistry 29 · Botany 19 · Zoology 15) as the classification target up front to prevent catch-all drift (the CDS/Foundation lesson). `question_kind='pyq'`; per-exam dedup (0038) scopes hashes by exam.
  - **Pipeline (`scripts/neet/`, mirrors CDS + Foundation):** `config.ts` (paper registry + `NEET_CHAPTERS` + `allowedSubjectsForNumber`) · `lib.ts` (**pure core, TDD `tests/neet-lib.test.ts` 22 cases**: `optionLetter` 1-4→A-D, `parseAnswer` "Answer (2)"/"Ans. (3)"/bare-number/letter → A-D, `normalizeQuestions` self-heal (options-object→array, difficulty synonyms, answer→letter), `buildRecords`, `validateRows` coverage/blank/one-correct/latex/collision) · `render.ts` (per-page PNGs; **two-column Re-NEET → L/R column split**) · `commit.ts` (globs `data/<paper>.*.json` part-files, `commitStaged` PRIVATE) · `attach-images.ts` (crops a **fractional bbox of the full PDF page** → `uploadImage` → `image_url`) · `flip-public.ts` (`--except=<n>` keeps flagged rows PRIVATE) · `preview.ts` (KaTeX review HTML).
  - **The load-bearing design decisions (user-approved):** (1) **options `(1)-(4)` map POSITIONALLY to A-D** — the booklet numbers options, the bank stores letters; `Answer (2)`→B. (2) **Biology is content-mixed under a single "BIOLOGY" banner** (NOT the official Botany-91-135 / Zoology-136-180 block split) → subject assigned **per-question by content** (plant→Botany, animal/human→Zoology; biotech-principles + evolution → Zoology to match the catalog; genetics/molecular/ecology → Botany). The Biology split lands ~Botany-heavy (2025 was 54/36) — a content classification, not the official 45/45 seat split. (3) **Keys transcribed verbatim, committed PRIVATE, spot-checked, then flipped PUBLIC** — the official coaching keys are trustworthy; the real risk is transcription/option-order error. (4) **worked solutions transcribed** (LaTeX) for /notes/RAG value.
  - **Execution: 4 parallel vision agents per paper** (Physics Q1-45, Chemistry Q46-90, Biology Q91-135, Biology Q136-180 — each writes its own `data/<paper>.<part>.json`; commit globs them). Validated on 2025 first (end-to-end → 180 PUBLIC), then 2026 + Re-NEET reusing the hardened pipeline. Every paper: 180/180 rows valid; the only recurring build flag was **subject↔chapter catalog mismatch** (biotech-principles filed Botany, evolution filed Botany) → auto-fixed by aligning subject to the chapter's catalog. Spot-checks (Q3/Q4/Q44/Q45/Q180 in 2025; Q46/Q97-100 in 2026; Q29/Q91-95 in Re-NEET) all matched the source keys.
  - **Figures: 68 cropped-and-attached** (Physics circuits/graphs dominate). The bbox pass = vision agents returning **full-page fractional bboxes** (display-independent, unlike pixel coords), cropped from the PDF page at 4×. Eyeball bboxes are imprecise → **always montage-verify the crops and re-tighten** (clipped tops / included Answer-text / wrong-page were the recurring errors; ~6-of-23 needed a second pass each paper). Option-graph/structure blocks (the OPTIONS are the figures) are attached as one crop; figures split across a page/column boundary (Re-NEET 2-column) or that are text-answerable were **dropped** (their text transcription suffices).
  - **Official-key edge cases, handled per "use the official key as source of truth":** dual keys **2025 Q63 (Answer 1,2)**, **2026 Q58 (Answer 3 or 4)**, **Re-NEET Q38 (Answer 2,3)** → stored the primary option + a solution note documenting the dual key, flipped PUBLIC. **Bonus/no-valid-option: Re-NEET Q26 (Vernier, computed 1.06 cm not among options → NTA declared Bonus)** → kept PRIVATE via `--except=26`. **2026 Q43 "Answer (2*)"** → kept verbatim as 2.
  - **Ops lessons banked** ([[neet-ingestion]] + `scripts/neet/README.md`): (a) the render PNGs are large — **`attach-images`/`commit` read from the PDF/JSON, not `out/`**, so delete a paper's `out/` dir after attaching; a mid-write **`ENOSPC` truncates a JSON to empty** (rebuilt the physics figures manifest from the agent's reported bboxes). (b) Two-column papers need a **separate full-page render** for the bbox agents (they must give full-page fractions; `render.ts`'s `_L`/`_R` split is for transcription only). Gate: full prepush PASS (typecheck·lint·notes·**test**·build); `npm run stats` → NEET 539, total PUBLIC **19,004**. **Committed + pushed + live** (base pipeline + `examContext.ts` picker in `07a3298`/`86c4fa8`; the 68 figures were then re-cropped + verified leak-free via the snapCrop tooling — see the 2026-07-01 figure entry above).

- **2026-07-01 — Source-verified `/solution-cleanup` of 7 MHT-CET Maths "Applications of Derivative" bank-extraction defects flagged during the notes build (against the on-disk 2023/2024 May shift QP+AK docx).** The authoring agents surfaced garbled options / muddled solutions; each was pandoc-verified against the source paper + its official answer key. **Verifying first flipped two of my own conclusions** (reconfirms [[mhtcet-source-docx-render]] + [[audit-probe-symmetry]]): (1) the **tank-cost** pair (`5b5da217`/`be13ddf4`) LOOKED wrong-keyed — physically the least cost is ₹3300 (base ₹900 + sides ₹2400), not the keyed ₹2400 — but the **official AK explicitly computes `C − C′ = 3300 − 900 = 2400`**, so ₹2400 (option C) is the official answer; **key C preserved**, solution rewritten honestly noting the official key omits the base (a paper defect, NOT a bank error). (2) the **poster** pair both have a source-paper typo (printed "8 m²" but the options + official solution use **18 m²**); official answer is **(a) 2√3, 3√3** for both years — so the 2024 `b6015937` (keyed A) was already right (solution rewritten), but the 2023 `42b03f34` was **wrong-keyed B → flipped to A** AND its options were mis-extracted (`2/√3` → `2√3`). Plus 3 pure option/stem-extraction fixes, keys already correct: `9faf2970` diagonal-square option D was garbled `\frac{1}{0}\sqrt2` (÷0) → `\frac{10}{\sqrt2}` + C → `\frac{1}{10\sqrt2}`; `20725666` parabola options C≡A, D≡B duplicated → `36/5`, `18/5`; `9148edb2` ladder stem had a **blank rate in the source paper itself** (AK answer 8/15 ⇒ rate = 1 m/sec) → filled + stem completed. All via a one-off with proper `content_hash` recompute + per-exam collision guard (the two posters stay distinct by a comma/period stem diff); exactly-one-correct verified on all 7. **One genuine wrong-key flip (`42b03f34` B→A)** adds to the bank-wide count. **Lesson: source-verify a suspected wrong key against the OFFICIAL AK before flipping — the "physically correct" answer can differ from the official key (tank), and a defect can be a source-paper typo (poster "8"→18, ladder blank rate) rather than a bank error.**

The other flags were **false alarms** — the three `f(x)=3x³−18x²+27x−40 on S` copies (`97164649`/`a10a4a4d`/`1e176136`) all correctly key to 122; the letters differ only because option ORDER differs per copy (reconfirms: re-derive every claimed "letter mismatch" before touching a key). Key flips via the existing `scripts/grounding/apply-key-fix.ts` (collision-guarded content_hash recompute).
  - **Authoring: 7-agent parallel batch + serial merge.** Each agent read the type defs + CET-Differentiation precedent + the same-topic NDA-AoD chapter, read its subtopic's bank SOLUTIONS, authored its file, and returned a 100%-coverage `(question_id, concept_slug)` mapping. Main agent wrote chapter.ts/index.ts/registry/wrappers + ran ONE 183-row tag INSERT (**183/183 tagged, 0 mistags** verified by a subtopic_slug→DB-name join). `cetaod-` concept-slug prefix (NDA owns `aod-`); `\log` convention. **Reused existing viz slugs** (`diff-tangent-slope` · `aod-sign-of-derivative` · `aod-extrema-curve` · `lines-angle-between-diagram`) on the spatial concepts — **zero new SVGs** (diagrams still 205), so no `VisualizationSlug`/`ConceptUnitCard` edits.
  - **notes-lint #5 caught 12 genuine worked-example/self-check == featured-PYQ dups** (the agents seeded several examples from the featured PYQ despite the "author WE first, then a DIFFERENT PYQ" instruction) → cleared via 6 parallel fix-agents, each re-authoring only its flagged examples to genuinely different problems with **independently-derived** answers. One residual was a **heuristic false positive** — a re-authored sin(31°) approximation still shared the universal 30°-table constants (0.8660, 0.0175) with the cos(30°30′) PYQ; re-anchored to sin(46°)/45° (no shared distinctive number) to clear it. Reconfirms [[notes-concept-content-alignment]] (#5 is number-overlap heuristic; the universal-constant false-positive is real — re-anchor to a different standard angle).
  - **Gates green:** typecheck · notes:lint (0 err / 0 warn for the chapter) · notes:latex (OK) · notes:order (7 subtopics synced, no name-join drift) · **quiz:coverage 46 concepts / 91 traps / 0 formula gaps** (quiz-ready by construction) · notes:coverage (no real technique gaps — only `\big(`/`\ln` notation false positives) · full prepush PASS; all 8 routes curl-verified HTTP 200 (landing is `ƒ` → smoke-tested per the build-blind-to-ƒ-pages pitfall). MHT-CET Maths now 5 of 27 chapters noted. Commit `3bbc267` (merge `38bb7fd`).

## Notes editorial workflow

The full step-by-step workflow for authoring a `/notes` chapter lives in **[NOTES_WORKFLOW.md](NOTES_WORKFLOW.md)** — **read it before building one.** It covers: Step 0 pedagogy-first structure (reshape the bank to the teaching arc; read HARD+MODERATE solutions; MHT-CET-Maths defaults) · Step 1 the `ConceptUnit` formula/reference discriminated union + the diagram decision rule + concept-content alignment · Step 1b author-for-clean-quiz-harvest · Step 2 the `NOTES_CHAPTERS` registry · Step 3 the chat tagging session (set-sibling co-location, 100% coverage) · Step 4 the gate chain (`notes:lint`/`notes:latex`/`notes:coverage`/`quiz:coverage`/`prepush`) · Step 5 ship + `npm run stats` + `notes:order` · the invariants to verify before commit.

## After a new upload

When a new question-batch lands, run the 6-step ritual in order. The upload pipeline is auto (taxonomy auto-creates, `content_hash` dedup, default PUBLIC visibility, live counts in `/browse` + dashboard + `/guide` TOP_11 + `/notes` drills). What's NOT auto and needs this ritual: **tag coverage, taxonomy fragmentation cleanup, and TS-static editorial copy**.

1. **Sanity-check the batch** (always) — confirm `inserted` vs PUBLIC/PRIVATE counts, PYQ metadata stamped, no missing years
2. **Taxonomy delta cleanup** (if ≥5 new subtopic auto-creates or a new chapter name) — Phase D delta inline; don't restart from scratch. Precedent: 2026-05-17 NDA Maths re-Phase-D (~770 new auto-creates closed in one session)
3. **Principle-tag delta survey** (if new questions in TOP_11 chapter footprints) — incremental per-new-chapter, not whole-bank
4. **Concept-tag delta session** (if new questions in `/notes`-covered subtopics) — also revisit dropped-gap ConceptUnits; bank growth can mature what was previously dropped
4b. **Notes-coverage check** (if new questions in a `/notes`-covered chapter) — `npm run notes:coverage <subjectRoute> <chapterSlug>` and triage the macro/technique channel. New uploads are how a *technique gap* drifts in (a fresh question using a method the notes don't teach); this is the routine catch for it. Add a concept for any real gap.
5. **Editorial copy refresh** (if counts move ≥5–10%) — `whyItMatters`, OVERVIEW constants, CHAPTER_TABLE, strategy %HARD, trends callouts. Mechanical TS edits
6. **Integrity check** (always) — `npm run notes:lint` + `npm run prepush` before commit

Steps 2–5 (incl. 4b) are conditional and can be deferred / queued across sessions. Full procedure + SQL probes + skip-conditions: [[post-upload-ritual]].

## Adding a new RLS-protected table

Follow the `questions`/`options` pattern (see `0002_rls.sql` + `0004_upload_staging.sql`):
1. Enable RLS.
2. Read policy: `org_id = private.current_user_org_id()`.
3. Write policy: same plus `private.current_user_is_admin()` if admin-curated.
4. Add a test in `tests/upload-rls.test.ts` (or new equivalent) proving a TEACHER cannot write and a member of another org cannot read.
5. Run `mcp__supabase__get_advisors` after applying the migration.
